Model before rewriting:

letting n be 8
letting AMOUNT_QUEENS be domain int(0..((n - 1)))
find q1: matrix indexed by [AMOUNT_QUEENS] of AMOUNT_QUEENS
find q2: matrix indexed by [AMOUNT_QUEENS] of int((-((n - 1)))..((n - 1)))
find q3: matrix indexed by [AMOUNT_QUEENS] of int(0..(product([(n - 1),2;int(1..)])))

such that

allDifferent(q1),
allDifferent(q2),
allDifferent(q3),
q1[0] = 0,
q2[0] = 0,
q3[0] = 0,
and([ and([and([and([q1[i] >= sum([q2[i],i;int(1..)]),q2[i] >= (q1[i] - i);int(1..)]),q3[i] >= sum([q1[i],i;int(1..)]);int(1..)]),q1[i] >= (q3[i] - i);int(1..)]) | i : AMOUNT_QUEENS ]),
and([ and([q2[i] <= ((n - 1) - i),q2[i] >= -(i);int(1..)]) | i : AMOUNT_QUEENS ]),
and([ and([q3[i] <= sum([i,(n - 1);int(1..)]),q3[i] >= i;int(1..)]) | i : AMOUNT_QUEENS ])
